# Deadline trades — Couch To Front Office v10.3

32 fresh Boston franchises. 436 AI-to-AI deadline trades. 188 incoming calls to Boston.

Ratings against real NHL deadline prices in `ref/MARKET.md`: **realistic 390**, **questionable 42**, **absurd 4**. The write-up of totals, patterns, the ten worst deals, and the trade-code causes is in `sims/ANALYSIS.md`.

Trades that include a 1st: 17. Trades that include two or more 1sts: 1. Trades that move a star (engine star, or 90+ OVR): 3.
